<h3 class="st"><a href="https://safedep.io/arrayref-proc-macro1-rust-build-time-malware/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malicious Rust crate Arrayref runs a build-time payload</span><span class='zh'>恶意 Rust crate Arrayref 在构建时载荷执行</span></a><span class="dom">safedep.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A build-time payload in the Arrayref crate demonstrates how cargo&#x27;s build scripts can run arbitrary code during compilation, creating supply-chain risks for Rust projects.</span><span class='zh'>Arrayref 的构建时载荷暴露了 cargo 构建脚本在编译阶段执行任意代码的风险，暴露了 Rust 项目的供应链隐患。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A build-time payload in the Arrayref crate demonstrates how cargo&#x27;s build scripts can run arbitrary code during compilation, creating supply-chain risks for Rust projects. The incident reinforces the need for careful dependency auditing, lockfiles, and tooling that detects unusual build behavior.</span><span class='zh'>Arrayref 的构建时载荷暴露了 cargo 构建脚本在编译阶段执行任意代码的风险，暴露了 Rust 项目的供应链隐患。事件强调对依赖项进行严格审计、锁定版本以及使用能检测异常构建行为的工具的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Amazon Mechanical Turk will shut down on Sept 30, ending a long-running crowdwork and data-labeling platform for ML.</span><span class='zh'>Amazon Mechanical Turk 将于9月30日停止运营。</span></p>

The move affects researchers and companies relying on microtasks, pushing them to alternatives or in-house labeling workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Amazon Mechanical Turk 将于9月30日停止运营。这一决策结束了长期用于众包任务和机器学习数据标注的平台，相关方需转向其他平台或自行开展标注工作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/world/us/meta-settles-with-us-states-over-social-media-harms-2026-08-26/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta reaches $17B settlement over social media harms to children</span><span class='zh'>Meta就儿童在社交媒体上的伤害达成170亿美元和解</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta agrees to a $17 billion settlement with U.S.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 与多州就其社交平台对儿童造成的有害影响达成170亿美元和解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta agrees to a $17 billion settlement with U.S. states over alleged harms to children from its social platforms. The deal includes funding for safety improvements, independent oversight, and a framework to curb risky design patterns affecting young users, underscoring regulatory pressure on big tech.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 与多州就其社交平台对儿童造成的有害影响达成170亿美元和解。和解包括加强安全措施、独立监督，以及限制有害设计模式的合规要求，体现了监管对大型科技公司的持续压力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://app.dealroom.co/news/feed/oracle-bans-ai-generated-code-from-openjdk-despite-ellison-s-claim-oracle-isn-t-writing-its-own-code" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Oracle bans AI-generated code from OpenJDK</span><span class='zh'>Oracle 禁止 OpenJDK 使用 AI 生成的代码</span></a><span class="dom">app.dealroom.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Oracle bans AI-generated code from OpenJDK.</span><span class='zh'>甲骨文宣布禁止把 AI 生成的代码用于 OpenJDK。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Oracle bans AI-generated code from OpenJDK. The policy signals a firm stance on machine-produced software and licensing risk, potentially complicating AI-assisted development in the Java ecosystem. It could force contributors to audit code provenance more carefully.</span><span class='zh'>甲骨文宣布禁止把 AI 生成的代码用于 OpenJDK。此举表明其在机器生成软件方面的严格治理，可能影响 Java 生态中对 AI 辅助编码的使用和许可安排，并促使开发者更加审查代码来源。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://0xcc.io/posts/omarchy-root-creds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Omarchy: Any User Process Can Escalate to Root</span><span class='zh'>Omarchy：任意用户进程可升级为 root</span></a><span class="dom">0xcc.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A vulnerability called Omarchy lets any user process escalate to root, effectively breaking privilege isolation.</span><span class='zh'>名为 Omarchy 的漏洞允许任意用户进程提权到 root，从而破坏权限隔离。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A vulnerability called Omarchy lets any user process escalate to root, effectively breaking privilege isolation. The issue underscores how fragile privilege boundaries can be and emphasizes the need for rapid patches, safer defaults, and stronger containment.</span><span class='zh'>名为 Omarchy 的漏洞允许任意用户进程提权到 root，从而破坏权限隔离。问题凸显了权限边界的脆弱性，强调需要迅速修补、采用更安全的默认设置，并加强对进程的容器化或沙箱保护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fzakaria.com/2026/08/23/your-executable-is-a-sqlite-database" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Executable Is a SQLite Database</span><span class='zh'>可执行文件就是一个 SQLite 数据库</span></a><span class="dom">fzakaria.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Treating an executable as a SQLite database reframes how we analyze and store binary code, enabling structured querying and tooling.</span><span class='zh'>把可执行程序当作 SQLite 数据库来看待，能以结构化的方式对二进制代码进行分析、查询和工具化操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Treating an executable as a SQLite database reframes how we analyze and store binary code, enabling structured querying and tooling. The perspective suggests new ways to inspect, modify, or reason about binaries without abandoning traditional formats.</span><span class='zh'>把可执行程序当作 SQLite 数据库来看待，能以结构化的方式对二进制代码进行分析、查询和工具化操作。这一视角可能带来新的二进制分析方法，促进对现有格式的补充性思考。</span></p>

This underlines edge computing feasibility with commodity devices.</span><span class='zh'>将手机作为服务器的实践，展示用消费级设备承载小型服务的可行性。讨论的权衡包括电池、存储、操作系统支持和网络暴露时的安全性，并给出保持轻量、响应快速的实用建议。这也凸显了边缘计算在普通设备上的潜力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI&#x27;s chief ethics officer resigns after less than a year, signaling volatility in steering responsible AI at a leading lab.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的伦理负责人在不到一年任期后离职，揭示这家研究机构在负责任 AI 的治理上面临的变动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI&#x27;s chief ethics officer resigns after less than a year, signaling volatility in steering responsible AI at a leading lab. The move may recalibrate internal governance and external trust around AI safety, policy, and company commitments.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的伦理负责人在不到一年任期后离职，揭示这家研究机构在负责任 AI 的治理上面临的变动。此举可能影响内部治理与外部对 AI 安全、政策与承诺的信任度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ankursethi.com/blog/prevent-cognitive-debt-by-manually-retyping-llm-generated-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Prevent cognitive debt by manually retyping LLM-generated code</span><span class='zh'>通过手动重新输入LLM生成的代码以避免认知负债</span></a><span class="dom">ankursethi.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Typing out LLM-generated code by hand helps developers verify logic and catch subtle errors, reducing cognitive debt from relying on machine output.</span><span class='zh'>通过手动重新输入LLM生成的代码来审查其逻辑，可以帮助开发者发现潜在的错误和偏差，降低因为依赖AI输出而产生的认知负担。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Typing out LLM-generated code by hand helps developers verify logic and catch subtle errors, reducing cognitive debt from relying on machine output. The piece argues for deliberate code validation as a guardrail for safer AI-assisted software.</span><span class='zh'>通过手动重新输入LLM生成的代码来审查其逻辑，可以帮助开发者发现潜在的错误和偏差，降低因为依赖AI输出而产生的认知负担。文章呼吁在AI辅助编码场景中加入更严格的代码核对与测试。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Coincident with the 2026 eclipse, a collection of live webcams aggregates feeds from observatories and public viewing sites.</span><span class='zh'>与2026年日食同时上线的多路直播摄像头汇集了来自观测站与公共观测点的画面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Coincident with the 2026 eclipse, a collection of live webcams aggregates feeds from observatories and public viewing sites. The roundup helps observers plan timings, compare streams, and prepare for potential bandwidth spikes during peak moments. It’s a handy resource for remote viewers who want real-time visuals without leaving home.</span><span class='zh'>与2026年日食同时上线的多路直播摄像头汇集了来自观测站与公共观测点的画面。该清单帮助观众规划观看时间、比较画面质量，并预测日食高光时段的带宽压力。对于希望在家远程观看的用户，这是一个实用的实时画面资源。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/qcvjkzcs7j74" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions and Pages are experiencing degraded availability</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 与 Pages 服务降级</span></a><span class="dom">githubstatus.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions and Pages experienced degraded availability due to an incident, disrupting CI workflows and site hosting.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 与 Pages 遭遇可用性降级，影响持续集成工作流和站点托管。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions and Pages experienced degraded availability due to an incident, disrupting CI workflows and site hosting. The incident underscores the risk of relying on critical dev tools and the need for rapid recovery.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 与 Pages 遭遇可用性降级，影响持续集成工作流和站点托管。事件凸显对关键开发工具的依赖风险及快速恢复的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49462253" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: PayPal blocks GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>HN 贴：PayPal 阻止 GrapheneOS</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PayPal blocks GrapheneOS, a privacy-focused Android fork, affecting users and highlighting tensions between large payment platforms and alternative mobile ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>据称 PayPal 阻止 GrapheneOS 用户使用其服务，凸显大型支付平台与隐私导向的操作系统之间的紧张关系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PayPal blocks GrapheneOS, a privacy-focused Android fork, affecting users and highlighting tensions between large payment platforms and alternative mobile ecosystems. This underscores concerns about platform interoperability, device compatibility, and vendor gatekeeping.</span><span class='zh'>据称 PayPal 阻止 GrapheneOS 用户使用其服务，凸显大型支付平台与隐私导向的操作系统之间的紧张关系。事件引发对跨平台兼容性、设备认证以及厂商封禁策略的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bradbury&#x27;s story about autonomous houses feels prophetic in today&#x27;s tech-saturated world. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://forum.level1techs.com/t/why-your-local-llm-feels-dumber-than-it-is/253917" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why your local LLM feels dumber than it is</span><span class='zh'>本地大模型为何看起来不那么聪明</span></a><span class="dom">forum.level1techs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A local LLM may feel less capable than a hosted model because of limited compute, memory, and latency constraints that force smaller context windows and cruder prompting.</span><span class='zh'>本地大模型因计算、内存和延迟受限，导致上下文窗口变小、提示粗糙，从而感知上不如云端模型智能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A local LLM may feel less capable than a hosted model because of limited compute, memory, and latency constraints that force smaller context windows and cruder prompting. The discussion covers practical ways to tune performance, including speed-accuracy trade-offs, quantization, and tool integration.</span><span class='zh'>本地大模型因计算、内存和延迟受限，导致上下文窗口变小、提示粗糙，从而感知上不如云端模型智能。内容给出提升性能的实际做法，包括速度与准确性的权衡、量化和工具集成。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1091231/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Debian votes to allow &quot;responsible use of generative AI&quot;</span><span class='zh'>Debian 决定允许生成式 AI 的负责任使用</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Debian voted to permit responsible use of generative AI in its workflows, with guardrails to address licensing and safety.</span><span class='zh'>Debian 投票同意在其工作流程中允许生成式 AI 的负责任使用，并设立守则以应对安全与许可等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Debian voted to permit responsible use of generative AI in its workflows, with guardrails to address licensing and safety. The decision signals how a major, community-driven distro balances AI-assisted tooling with trust and reproducibility in open source.</span><span class='zh'>Debian 投票同意在其工作流程中允许生成式 AI 的负责任使用，并设立守则以应对安全与许可等问题。这体现出开源社区在 AI 辅助开发中寻求平衡，既利用新工具也维护可重复性与信任基础。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/security/how-google-is-making-private-ai-practical-with-homomorphic-encryption/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google is making private AI practical with homomorphic encryption</span><span class='zh'>谷歌通过同态加密让私有 AI 变得可实用</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google is pursuing practical private AI by using homomorphic encryption to run computations on encrypted data.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌正通过同态加密让隐私保护的 AI 更具可用性，能够在加密数据上执行推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google is pursuing practical private AI by using homomorphic encryption to run computations on encrypted data. This approach aims to unlock privacy-preserving AI in cloud and on-device settings, though it remains challenging in terms of performance.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌正通过同态加密让隐私保护的 AI 更具可用性，能够在加密数据上执行推理。该思路有望在云端和本地场景中保护用户数据隐私，但在性能方面仍存在挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alz-journals.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1002/dad2.70432" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Semaglutide linked to lower predicted dementia risk</span><span class='zh'>司美格鲁肽与预测性痴呆风险下降相关</span></a><span class="dom">alz-journals.onlinelibrary.wiley.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study finds that semaglutide exposure is associated with lower predicted dementia risk, hinting at links between metabolic health and brain aging.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示司美格鲁肽暴露与较低的预测性痴呆风险相关，提示代谢健康与脑部老化之间可能存在联系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study finds that semaglutide exposure is associated with lower predicted dementia risk, hinting at links between metabolic health and brain aging. While intriguing, the finding is observational and needs controlled trials to establish causality and understand mechanisms.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示司美格鲁肽暴露与较低的预测性痴呆风险相关，提示代谢健康与脑部老化之间可能存在联系。该发现为观测性结论，需通过对照试验来确立因果关系并揭示潜在机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/a-civilian-plane-crashed-in-new-mexico-was-the-militarys-tech-to-blame/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Civilian plane crash in New Mexico tied to military GPS blocking</span><span class='zh'>新墨西哥民用飞机坠毁与军事GPS屏蔽相关</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A civilian plane crash in New Mexico has been linked to military GPS disruptions or deliberate jamming.</span><span class='zh'>新墨西哥的一起民用飞机坠毁事件被关联到军事GPS干扰或阻断。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A civilian plane crash in New Mexico has been linked to military GPS disruptions or deliberate jamming. The incident highlights how reliance on GPS for aviation navigation can be vulnerable to adversarial tech, raising safety and policy questions about airspace interference. It underscores the need for better GPS resilience or backup navigation in civilian aviation.</span><span class='zh'>新墨西哥的一起民用飞机坠毁事件被关联到军事GPS干扰或阻断。此事凸显民用航空对GPS的高度依赖在对手干扰下的脆弱性，并引发关于空域干扰的安全与政策讨论，强调需要提升GPS韧性或采用备用导航方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antirez/h3.c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>H3-metal – Native MiniMax-H3 inference for Apple Silicon</span><span class='zh'>H3-metal — 面向 Apple Silicon 的本地 MiniMax-H3 推理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Native MiniMax-H3 inference runs directly on Apple Silicon via H3-metal.</span><span class='zh'>H3-metal 将 MiniMax-H3 的推理本地化到 Apple Silicon，借助 Metal 加速实现更高效的推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Native MiniMax-H3 inference runs directly on Apple Silicon via H3-metal. It targets efficient, GPU-accelerated performance for H3 workloads on macOS, reducing cross-OS friction and CPU load. This is relevant for developers who want local, fast inference on Macs.</span><span class='zh'>H3-metal 将 MiniMax-H3 的推理本地化到 Apple Silicon，借助 Metal 加速实现更高效的推理。目标是在 macOS 上提供更高效的推理性能，减少跨平台移植的负担和 CPU 开销。对于需要在 Mac 上本地快速处理 H3 相关工作流的开发者尤为有用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Lakr233/vphone-cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Boot a Virtual iPhone via Apple&#x27;s Virtualization.framework</span><span class='zh'>通过苹果的 Virtualization.framework 启动虚拟 iPhone</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hobbyist demonstrates how to boot a virtual iPhone using Apple&#x27;s Virtualization.framework, effectively running iOS as a VM on a host.</span><span class='zh'>开发者通过苹果的 Virtualization.framework 展示了如何引导一个虚拟 iPhone，在宿主机上运行 iOS 的虚拟机。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hobbyist demonstrates how to boot a virtual iPhone using Apple&#x27;s Virtualization.framework, effectively running iOS as a VM on a host. The project shows the feasibility of isolating and testing iOS software without real hardware, unlocking new avenues for development, debugging, and security research. It remains experimental and depends on sensitive tooling and compliance considerations.</span><span class='zh'>开发者通过苹果的 Virtualization.framework 展示了如何引导一个虚拟 iPhone，在宿主机上运行 iOS 的虚拟机。该项目证明了在无实体设备的情况下测试和调试 iOS 的可行性，为开发、安全研究及隔离测试开辟新路径。此实现尚属实验性，且涉及工具与合规风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DenisSergeevitch/desktop-fly" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 3D fruit fly on macOS desktop powered by the real FlyWire connectome</span><span class='zh'>以真實 FlyWire 連接體為動力的 macOS 桌面 3D 果蠅</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This macOS widget renders a 3D fruit‑fly on the desktop whose neuronal activity is driven by the FlyWire connectome data, turning real connectomic wiring into live visual behavior.</span><span class='zh'>這個 macOS 小工具在桌面上呈現一隻 3D 果蠅，其神經活動由 FlyWire 連接體資料驅動，將真實的連接體網絡轉化為即時可視的行為。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This macOS widget renders a 3D fruit‑fly on the desktop whose neuronal activity is driven by the FlyWire connectome data, turning real connectomic wiring into live visual behavior. It&#x27;s a playful but impressive demo of making scientific connectome datasets interactive and tangible on a personal computer.</span><span class='zh'>這個 macOS 小工具在桌面上呈現一隻 3D 果蠅，其神經活動由 FlyWire 連接體資料驅動，將真實的連接體網絡轉化為即時可視的行為。這是把科學連接體資料互動化並帶到個人電腦上的趣味而令人印象深刻的示範。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/rosenbridge" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hardware backdoors in some x86 CPUs</span><span class='zh'>某些 x86 CPU 可能存在硬件后门</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Rosenbridge project points to potential hardware backdoors in certain x86 CPUs, illustrating how microarchitectural features could be misused.</span><span class='zh'>Rosenbridge 项目指出某些 x86 CPU 可能存在硬件后门，说明微架构层面的特征可能被滥用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Rosenbridge project points to potential hardware backdoors in certain x86 CPUs, illustrating how microarchitectural features could be misused. The finding underscores supply-chain security risks and the importance of firmware and chipset transparency.</span><span class='zh'>Rosenbridge 项目指出某些 x86 CPU 可能存在硬件后门，说明微架构层面的特征可能被滥用。此发现强调了供应链安全风险，以及固件与芯片组透明度的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/leonickson1/Swiftlet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Run an 80B Qwen in 4.3 GB of RAM on a Mac, and a 35B on an iPhone</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在 Mac 4.3 GB RAM 条件下跑 80B 的 Qwen，在 iPhone 上跑 35B</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on project demonstrates running large language models on consumer hardware, with an 80B Qwen on a Mac using 4.3 GB RAM and a 35B variant on an iPhone.</span><span class='zh'>一个实操项目展示在普通硬件上运行大型语言模型：在 Mac 上用 4.3 GB RAM 运行 80B 的 Qwen，在 iPhone 上运行 35B。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on project demonstrates running large language models on consumer hardware, with an 80B Qwen on a Mac using 4.3 GB RAM and a 35B variant on an iPhone. The approach relies on memory-efficient quantization and optimization to enable on-device inference, reducing cloud dependence and latency.</span><span class='zh'>一个实操项目展示在普通硬件上运行大型语言模型：在 Mac 上用 4.3 GB RAM 运行 80B 的 Qwen，在 iPhone 上运行 35B。该方法依赖低内存量化与优化，使本地推理成为可能，降低对云端的依赖并降低延迟。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/trycua/cua/blob/main/blog/gpu-passthrough-macos-vms.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple Silicon and macOS VMs: Faster LLM Inference with llama.cpp</span><span class='zh'>Apple Silicon 与 macOS 虚拟机：借助 llama.cpp 实现更快的 LLM 推理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple Silicon machines running macOS VMs can use GPU passthrough to speed up LLM inference with llama.cpp.</span><span class='zh'>在 Apple Silicon 的 Mac 上，通过为 macOS 虚拟机启用 GPU 直通，可以用 llama.cpp 加速大语言模型推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple Silicon machines running macOS VMs can use GPU passthrough to speed up LLM inference with llama.cpp. The setup shows how Macs can squeeze more AI throughput for local or private-cloud workflows, though it comes with VM stability and driver caveats. Practical tips and pitfalls accompany the approach for developers exploring on-device AI.</span><span class='zh'>在 Apple Silicon 的 Mac 上，通过为 macOS 虚拟机启用 GPU 直通，可以用 llama.cpp 加速大语言模型推理。这种方案展示了在 Mac 上提升 AI 处理能力的可行性，便于实现本地化或私有云推理。实施中的现实挑战包括虚拟机稳定性、驱动兼容性以及功耗与散热等问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zachahn/vomit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Vomit: Clean up Claude 5&#x27;s token output with a separate LLM</span><span class='zh'>Vomit：用单独的 LLM 清理 Claude 5 的输出</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vomit proposes using a separate LLM to post-process Claude 5&#x27;s token output, aiming to reduce hallucinations and improve reliability.</span><span class='zh'>Vomit 提出用独立的语言模型对 Claude 5 的输出进行后处理，以降低幻觉率并提高可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vomit proposes using a separate LLM to post-process Claude 5&#x27;s token output, aiming to reduce hallucinations and improve reliability. The approach illustrates how modular safety pipelines can complement the base model.</span><span class='zh'>Vomit 提出用独立的语言模型对 Claude 5 的输出进行后处理，以降低幻觉率并提高可靠性。这一做法展示了模块化安全管线如何与基础模型协同提升安全性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RyanCodrai/turbovec" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Turbovec – Google&#x27;s TurboQuant for vector search in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Turbovec：用 Rust 實作的 Google TurboQuant 向量搜尋</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turbovec is a Rust implementation of TurboQuant for compressed vector search, aiming to speed up nearest‑neighbor queries with lower memory footprints.</span><span class='zh'>Turbovec 是用 Rust 實作的 TurboQuant 向量搜尋庫，致力於在降低記憶體使用的同時加速最近鄰查詢。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turbovec is a Rust implementation of TurboQuant for compressed vector search, aiming to speed up nearest‑neighbor queries with lower memory footprints. It&#x27;s useful for embedding search workflows that need efficient on‑disk or in‑memory storage with fast distance computations in production systems.</span><span class='zh'>Turbovec 是用 Rust 實作的 TurboQuant 向量搜尋庫，致力於在降低記憶體使用的同時加速最近鄰查詢。對需在生產環境中以高效方式儲存嵌入並快速計算距離的應用來說十分實用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3494" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RipGrep musl binaries occasionally segfault during very-large searches</span><span class='zh'>RipGrep 的 musl 二进制在极大规模搜索时偶发崩溃</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bug causes occasional segfaults in musl-built ripgrep during very large searches.</span><span class='zh'>在极大规模的搜索中，musl 构建的 ripgrep 出现偶发性崩溃。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bug causes occasional segfaults in musl-built ripgrep during very large searches. It points to edge-case interactions between the binary, musl libc, and heavy search workloads, affecting users on musl-based systems. Maintainers are investigating; workarounds may include avoiding extremely large searches or using a glibc build.</span><span class='zh'>在极大规模的搜索中，musl 构建的 ripgrep 出现偶发性崩溃。问题指向二进制、musl libc 与高负载搜索场景之间的边缘情况，影响使用 musl 的发行版用户。维护者正在调查，临时解决办法可能包括避免极大规模搜索或改用基于 glibc 的版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/systemd/systemd/issues/40262" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Single log line is 49KB+ (ext4) / 110KB+ (btrfs) of systemd-journald disk writes</span><span class='zh'>systemd 日志单行在 ext4/btrfs 下的极大写入</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Notes that a single systemd-journald log line can trigger very large disk writes on ext4 and btrfs.</span><span class='zh'>指出 systemd-journald 的单条日志行在 ext4 下会触发极大的磁盘写入，在 btrfs 下也如此。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Notes that a single systemd-journald log line can trigger very large disk writes on ext4 and btrfs. This has implications for log throughput, storage planning, and how journaling backends should handle extreme entries.</span><span class='zh'>指出 systemd-journald 的单条日志行在 ext4 下会触发极大的磁盘写入，在 btrfs 下也如此。这对日志吞吐、存储规划以及日志后端处理极端日志条目提出了考虑。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust-project-goals/blob/main/src/2026/move-trait.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust-project-goals/blob/main/src/2026/move-trait.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rust project goals: Immobile types and guaranteed destructors</span><span class='zh'>Rust 项目目标：不可移动的类型与保证析构函数</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Rust project goals discuss immobile (unmovable) types and guaranteed destructors, likely addressing pinned types and safe drop semantics to enable safer self-referential and low-level abstractions.</span><span class='zh'>文档聚焦不可移动类型与保证析构，涉及固定型与安全析构语义，旨在提升自引用及底层抽象的安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Rust project goals discuss immobile (unmovable) types and guaranteed destructors, likely addressing pinned types and safe drop semantics to enable safer self-referential and low-level abstractions.</span><span class='zh'>文档聚焦不可移动类型与保证析构，涉及固定型与安全析构语义，旨在提升自引用及底层抽象的安全性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/stemdeckapp/stemdeck" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>StemDeck, a free, open-source and local AI stem separator</span><span class='zh'>StemDeck：免费开源、在本地运行的 AI 音轨分离工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>StemDeck offers free, open-source AI-driven audio stem separation that runs locally on your machine.</span><span class='zh'>StemDeck 提供免费、开源的 AI 音轨分离工具，能够在本地计算机上离线运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>StemDeck offers free, open-source AI-driven audio stem separation that runs locally on your machine. By processing offline, it avoids cloud privacy concerns and can leverage your hardware for faster results, making it attractive for musicians and audio engineers exploring stem remixing.</span><span class='zh'>StemDeck 提供免费、开源的 AI 音轨分离工具，能够在本地计算机上离线运行。避免云端传输带来的隐私风险，并能充分利用本地硬件提升处理速度，成为音乐人和音频工程师进行分轨再混音的有力工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Universal-Debloater-Alliance/universal-android-debloater-next-generation/issues/1426" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>On non-rooted Android 17, ADB uninstall of system apps fails</span><span class='zh'>在非 root 的 Android 17 上，ADB 卸载系统应用失败</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>On non-rooted Android 17 devices, ADB cannot uninstall system apps.</span><span class='zh'>在未获得 root 的 Android 17 设备上，使用 ADB 卸载系统应用会失败。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>On non-rooted Android 17 devices, ADB cannot uninstall system apps. The limitation highlights how device integrity and built-in protections constrain user customization, affecting bloatware removal and device ownership.</span><span class='zh'>在未获得 root 的 Android 17 设备上，使用 ADB 卸载系统应用会失败。这体现了设备安全性对自定义能力的限制，去除预装应用等需求将受阻，除非使用 root 或厂商工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-python/blob/main/httpx2.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Migrating to HTTPX2</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI：迁移到 HTTPX2</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI is migrating its Python client to HTTPX2, replacing the older HTTP client.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Python 客户端迁移至 HTTPX2，替代原有的 HTTP 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI is migrating its Python client to HTTPX2, replacing the older HTTP client. The upgrade aims to boost performance and streaming reliability, but developers may encounter breaking changes and must align their dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Python 客户端迁移至 HTTPX2，替代原有的 HTTP 客户端。此升级旨在提升性能与流式传输的稳定性，但开发者可能会遇到破坏性变更，需要同步更新依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/66504" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Session URL appended to commit messages and PR descriptions by default</span><span class='zh'>Claude 会话 URL 默认附加到提交信息和 PR 描述</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A default behavior in Claude code tools automatically appends the session URL to commit messages and pull request descriptions.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 相关工具默认会将会话 URL 附加到提交信息与 PR 描述中，存在隐私泄露风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A default behavior in Claude code tools automatically appends the session URL to commit messages and pull request descriptions. This poses potential privacy leakage risks, and maintainers may need to disable or patch the feature.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 相关工具默认会将会话 URL 附加到提交信息与 PR 描述中，存在隐私泄露风险。维护者可能需要禁用或修补此功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pg83/solo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solo – a .so loader for static Linux binaries</span><span class='zh'>Solo：一个用于静态 Linux 二进制的 .so 加载器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solo is a small tool that lets you load .so dynamic libraries into static Linux binaries, enabling fully self-contained executables.</span><span class='zh'>Solo 是一个小型工具，可将 .so 动态库装入静态 Linux 二进制文件，生成完全集成的可执行文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solo is a small tool that lets you load .so dynamic libraries into static Linux binaries, enabling fully self-contained executables. It simplifies distribution and can improve portability in environments with strict library policies.</span><span class='zh'>Solo 是一个小型工具，可将 .so 动态库装入静态 Linux 二进制文件，生成完全集成的可执行文件。它简化了分发并在对库政策严格的环境中提升了可移植性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/smiiiiiiiiiiiiiiii" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Exploiting System Management Mode with a very long interrupt</span><span class='zh'>利用极长中断攻击系统管理模式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proof-of-concept demonstrates how extremely long System Management Mode (SMM) interrupts can be abused to escalate privileges or leak data from firmware.</span><span class='zh'>一个概念性演示显示，极长的系统管理模式中断可能被用于提升权限或从固件窃取数据，暴露了固件层面的安全风险并强调需要在现代 CPU 中实现更强的隔离。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proof-of-concept demonstrates how extremely long System Management Mode (SMM) interrupts can be abused to escalate privileges or leak data from firmware. The work underscores firmware security risks and the need for robust isolation in modern CPUs.</span><span class='zh'>一个概念性演示显示，极长的系统管理模式中断可能被用于提升权限或从固件窃取数据，暴露了固件层面的安全风险并强调需要在现代 CPU 中实现更强的隔离。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kingroryg/turbokv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TurboKV: Insanely fast Rust key-value store</span><span class='zh'>TurboKV：极快的 Rust 键值存储</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TurboKV is a Rust-based key-value store touted for blazing speed, likely employing lock-free data structures and modern memory strategies.</span><span class='zh'>TurboKV 是基于 Rust 的高性能键值存储，主打极致速度，可能采用无锁设计和最新的内存管理策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TurboKV is a Rust-based key-value store touted for blazing speed, likely employing lock-free data structures and modern memory strategies. It targets high-throughput workloads for databases and caches, offering a practical signal of Rust&#x27;s viability for storage engines.</span><span class='zh'>TurboKV 是基于 Rust 的高性能键值存储，主打极致速度，可能采用无锁设计和最新的内存管理策略。它面向数据库和缓存等高吞吐场景，提供了 Rust 驱动存储引擎的新参考。</span></p>

This exposes cross-architecture emulation opportunities and prompts discussions about performance, legality, and ecosystem boundaries.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示了如何在 Apple Vision Pro 上运行 Android ARM64 的 VR 应用包，通过跨架构实现兼容性。该方法揭示了仿真/移植的瓶颈，同时也引发对性能、许可与可用性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/murerkinn/bookshelf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bookshelf – Self-hosted eBook library that runs on object storage</span><span class='zh'>Bookshelf – 自托管对象存储上的电子书库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bookshelf offers a self-hosted eBook library that runs on object storage, delivering scalable, private access to your digital library with search and metadata features.</span><span class='zh'>Bookshelf 提供一个在对象存储上运行的自托管电子书库，支持可扩展性与隐私化的数字藏书访问，并具备搜索与元数据功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pg83/shitty" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Shitty – fast terminal. Memory-unsafe and faster than yours</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Shitty——快速的终端，内存不安全且比你用的更快</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shitty is a fast, memory-unsafe terminal aimed at squeezing maximum speed, trading safety for performance.</span><span class='zh'>Shitty 是一个追求极致速度、且不保证内存安全的快速终端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shitty is a fast, memory-unsafe terminal aimed at squeezing maximum speed, trading safety for performance. It&#x27;s a provocative proof-of-concept that highlights what you can achieve by stripping away safety checks, while cautioning against using it in production.</span><span class='zh'>Shitty 是一个追求极致速度、且不保证内存安全的快速终端。通过牺牲安全性来提升性能，这个概念验证展示了在剥离安全检查后能达到的极端性能，同时提示在生产环境中不要使用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Git-knife provides a spreadsheet-like interface to edit commit messages, authors, and dates across a repository.</span><span class='zh'>Git-knife 提供像电子表格一样的界面，方便在整个仓库中编辑提交信息、作者和日期。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AntigmaLabs/ante" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Ante, a coding agent in a single binary that runs offline</span><span class='zh'>Ante：离线单二进制的编程代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ante packages a coding agent into a single binary that runs offline, enabling local code completion and automation without network access.</span><span class='zh'>Ante 将编码代理打包成一个单独的二进制文件实现离线运行，支持本地代码补全与自动化。</span></p>

This design minimizes external dependencies and enhances privacy, though updates may be less frequent.</span><span class='zh'>Ante 将编码代理打包成一个单独的二进制文件实现离线运行，支持本地代码补全与自动化。该设计降低对外部依赖、提升隐私，但更新可能不如在线方案频繁。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/fregacmols/RotaryCell" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RotaryCell: Making an unmodified rotary phone work over LTE with an ESP32-S3</span><span class='zh'>RotaryCell：让未修改的旋转拨号电话在 LTE 上工作</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RotaryCell documents hooking up an unmodified rotary phone to LTE using an ESP32-S3, bridging vintage telephony with modern networks.</span><span class='zh'>RotaryCell 记录如何用 ESP32-S3 将未改动的旋转拨号电话接入 LTE，连接传统电话与现代蜂窝网络。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RotaryCell documents hooking up an unmodified rotary phone to LTE using an ESP32-S3, bridging vintage telephony with modern networks. The project exposes the challenges and opportunities of reviving legacy devices in a cellular world.</span><span class='zh'>RotaryCell 记录如何用 ESP32-S3 将未改动的旋转拨号电话接入 LTE，连接传统电话与现代蜂窝网络。此项目凸显了对遗留设备重新利用的技术挑战与机遇。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pure-C implementation of MicroGPT achieves 10 million tokens per second on Apple M5 hardware, underscoring impressive runtime efficiency and the potential for lightweight AI inference on consumer devices.</span><span class='zh'>纯 C 实现的 MicroGPT 在苹果 M5 硬件上达到 1,000 万 tokens/秒，凸显低级语言实现下的高效推理能力，点亮边缘 AI 的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pure-C implementation of MicroGPT achieves 10 million tokens per second on Apple M5 hardware, underscoring impressive runtime efficiency and the potential for lightweight AI inference on consumer devices. This demonstrates how language models can be ported to low-level, high-performance stacks, enabling edge AI use cases.</span><span class='zh'>纯 C 实现的 MicroGPT 在苹果 M5 硬件上达到 1,000 万 tokens/秒，凸显低级语言实现下的高效推理能力，点亮边缘 AI 的潜力。此成就展示了将语言模型移植到高性能、低开销的栈上的可行性，便于在本地设备部署。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pydantic/httpx2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HTTPX2 – A next-generation HTTP client for Python</span><span class='zh'>HTTPX2：Python 的下一代 HTTP 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HTTPX2 proposes to advance Python HTTP client capabilities with features like faster async I/O, streaming, and better API ergonomics.</span><span class='zh'>HTTPX2 提议在 Python HTTP 客户端功能上进一步优化，包括更快的异步 IO、流式传输与更友好的 API。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HTTPX2 proposes to advance Python HTTP client capabilities with features like faster async I/O, streaming, and better API ergonomics. It aims to improve reliability and performance for modern Python apps.</span><span class='zh'>HTTPX2 提议在 Python HTTP 客户端功能上进一步优化，包括更快的异步 IO、流式传输与更友好的 API。目标是提升现代 Python 应用的可靠性与性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GrieferPig/esp32-s31-linux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Linux on ESP32</span><span class='zh'>在 ESP32 上运行 Linux</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bold experiment ports a Linux kernel to the ESP32-S31 microcontroller, showing the limits of running a full OS on ultra-low-power hardware.</span><span class='zh'>在极低功耗硬件上尝试将 Linux 内核移植到 ESP32-S31，展示了完整操作系统在此类设备上的边界与挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bold experiment ports a Linux kernel to the ESP32-S31 microcontroller, showing the limits of running a full OS on ultra-low-power hardware. The effort highlights the engineering hurdles—memory constraints, boot, drivers—and what it means for edge computing experiments and hobbyist hardware hacking. Not production-ready, but a milestone in expanding where Linux can run.</span><span class='zh'>在极低功耗硬件上尝试将 Linux 内核移植到 ESP32-S31，展示了完整操作系统在此类设备上的边界与挑战。该工作揭示了记忆体约束、启动与驱动等工程难题，以及这对边缘计算实验和爱好者硬件 Black 盒的意义。虽然尚未达到生产就绪，但标志着 Linux 可运行范围的扩展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/thiagotigaz/ocr-it" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OCR It – pull text out of un-copyable documents for your LLM</span><span class='zh'>OCR It：从不可复制的文档中提取文本供LLM使用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OCR It is an open-source tool that extracts text from documents that resist copying, feeding the results into LLM workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OCR It 是一个开源工具，能够从难以复制的文档中提取文本，并将结果用于LLM工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OCR It is an open-source tool that extracts text from documents that resist copying, feeding the results into LLM workflows. It supports scanned images and PDFs to unlock content for AI prompts and data processing. The project lowers the barrier to using non-editable sources in AI-enabled pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>OCR It 是一个开源工具，能够从难以复制的文档中提取文本，并将结果用于LLM工作流。它支持对扫描图像和不可编辑的PDF进行OCR处理，解锁内容供AI输入和数据处理。该项目降低了在AI工作流中使用受限文本的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rochus-keller/OberonSystem/tree/op2-rv32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Project Oberon System version running on RISC-V instead of RISC-5</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Oberon 系统在 RISC-V 上的实现，取代 RISC-5</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental Show HN post shows a version of the Project Oberon system running on RISC-V, rather than the previously targeted RISC-5.</span><span class='zh'>一个实验性的 Show HN 帖子展示了 Project Oberon 系统的一个版本在 RISC-V 上运行，而非传统的 RISC-5。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental Show HN post shows a version of the Project Oberon system running on RISC-V, rather than the previously targeted RISC-5. It demonstrates that a compact, educational OS can be ported to modern architectures, offering a learning resource for hobbyists. The entry may inspire emulation and education around language-oriented OS design.</span><span class='zh'>一个实验性的 Show HN 帖子展示了 Project Oberon 系统的一个版本在 RISC-V 上运行，而非传统的 RISC-5。它证明了一个紧凑、以教育为导向的操作系统可以移植到现代架构，为爱好者提供学习资源。此举可能激发对语言与操作系统设计的爱好与探索。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Demonstrates a pathway to fine-tune an 8B-parameter model on consumer hardware using efficient methods like LoRA and quantization.</span><span class='zh'>展示在消费级硬件上利用高效方法（如 LoRA 与量化）对一个 8B 参数模型进行微调的可行性，凸显大模型实验的民主化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Demonstrates a pathway to fine-tune an 8B-parameter model on consumer hardware using efficient methods like LoRA and quantization. This underscores the democratization of large-model experimentation, while acknowledging trade-offs in speed and accuracy on limited hardware.</span><span class='zh'>展示在消费级硬件上利用高效方法（如 LoRA 与量化）对一个 8B 参数模型进行微调的可行性，凸显大模型实验的民主化。与此同时，也要正视在有限硬件条件下可能带来的速度与精度折衷。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/swimmingbrain/texbrain" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TeXbrain, a LaTeX editor that runs pdfTeX in the browser via WASM</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: TeXbrain——在浏览器中通过 WASM 运行 pdfTeX 的 LaTeX 编辑器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TeXbrain brings pdfTeX to the browser via WebAssembly, turning any browser into a self contained LaTeX editor with live previews.</span><span class='zh'>TeXbrain 将 pdfTeX 通过 WebAssembly 在浏览器中执行，实现在浏览器内的 LaTeX 编辑和实时预览。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TeXbrain brings pdfTeX to the browser via WebAssembly, turning any browser into a self contained LaTeX editor with live previews. This client side approach eliminates server dependencies and could simplify teaching and rapid prototyping, though performance depends on memory constraints.</span><span class='zh'>TeXbrain 将 pdfTeX 通过 WebAssembly 在浏览器中执行，实现在浏览器内的 LaTeX 编辑和实时预览。这一客户端方案无需服务器即可工作，便于教学与快速原型开发，但大型文档的性能与内存限制需关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/runetes/maiao" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Maiao: Gerrit-style code review workflow for GitHub, GitLab, Gitea, others</span><span class='zh'>Maiao：面向 GitHub、GitLab、Gitea 等的 Gerrit 风格代码评审工作流</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Maiao brings Gerrit inspired code review into a portable workflow that spans GitHub, GitLab, Gitea, and others.</span><span class='zh'>Maiao 将 Gerrit 风格的代码评审工作流带到跨 GitHub、GitLab、Gitea 等平台的环境中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Maiao brings Gerrit inspired code review into a portable workflow that spans GitHub, GitLab, Gitea, and others. With a centralized set of review and approval rules, it lets teams enforce consistent policies across platforms and streamline cross repo collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Maiao 将 Gerrit 风格的代码评审工作流带到跨 GitHub、GitLab、Gitea 等平台的环境中。通过可移植的配置，统一评审、批准与 CI 触发等流程，帮助团队在多个平台间保持一致的代码质量与协作效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DioxusLabs/taffy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Taffy: A flexible, high-performance, cross-platform UI layout library</span><span class='zh'>Taffy：一个灵活高性能的跨平台 UI 布局库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Taffy is a fast, flexible cross-platform UI layout library from DioxusLabs, designed to deliver deterministic layouts across desktop, mobile, and embedded targets.</span><span class='zh'>Taffy 是 DioxusLabs 推出的一个快速、灵活的跨平台 UI 布局库，旨在在桌面、移动和嵌入式设备上提供确定性的布局。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Taffy is a fast, flexible cross-platform UI layout library from DioxusLabs, designed to deliver deterministic layouts across desktop, mobile, and embedded targets. Built with performance in mind, it aims to rival existing layout engines and improve the responsiveness of UI toolchains in Rust ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Taffy 是 DioxusLabs 推出的一个快速、灵活的跨平台 UI 布局库，旨在在桌面、移动和嵌入式设备上提供确定性的布局。以高性能为目标，它意在与现有布局引擎竞争，提升 Rust 生态中的 UI 构建链的响应速度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rails/solid_queue/releases/tag/v1.6.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 now supports fiber workers</span><span class='zh'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 现已支持纤程工作者</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 adds fiber-based workers, enabling lightweight concurrency inside the queue system.</span><span class='zh'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 增加了基于纤程的工作者，允许在队列系统内部实现轻量级并发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 adds fiber-based workers, enabling lightweight concurrency inside the queue system. This improves throughput for asynchronous workloads and reduces the need for OS threads, benefiting Ruby/Rails ecosystems and other event-driven stacks. The update makes writing scalable, non-blocking code simpler.</span><span class='zh'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 增加了基于纤程的工作者，允许在队列系统内部实现轻量级并发。提升异步工作负载的吞吐量，减少对操作系统线程的依赖，对 Ruby/Rails 生态和其他事件驱动框架尤其有利。本次更新让编写可扩展、非阻塞代码更为简单。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/johnsonjh/cpm386" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CP/M-386 – CP/M for 386 protected mode, derived from CP/M‑68K</span><span class='zh'>CP/M-386：386 保护模式下的 CP/M，源自 CP/M‑68K</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CP/M-386 brings CP/M to 386-class hardware in protected mode, derived from CP/M-68K.</span><span class='zh'>CP/M-386 将经典 CP/M 移植到 386 架构的保护模式，源自 CP/M‑68K。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CP/M-386 brings CP/M to 386-class hardware in protected mode, derived from CP/M-68K. It serves as a nostalgic platform for retro computing and a testbed for understanding legacy OS concepts on modern CPUs.</span><span class='zh'>CP/M-386 将经典 CP/M 移植到 386 架构的保护模式，源自 CP/M‑68K。它为复古计算爱好者提供一个怀旧平台，同时帮助学习在现代 CPU 上实现遗留操作系统的思路。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact, educational project that implements a GPT-2 model within a CMake-centric workflow. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/carpdiem/ember" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Ember – Redshift safe color palettes</span><span class='zh'>Ember——适用于 Redshift 的安全调色板</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ember is a collection of color palettes designed to be safe for Redshift rendering, emphasizing colorblind accessibility and perceptual uniformity.</span><span class='zh'>Ember 提供一组适合 Redshift 渲染的调色板，强调色盲友好与感知一致性，帮助数据可视化设计者避免误导性配色并快速选用现成配色。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ember is a collection of color palettes designed to be safe for Redshift rendering, emphasizing colorblind accessibility and perceptual uniformity. It helps data viz creators avoid misleading palettes and speeds up choosing ready-to-use schemes.</span><span class='zh'>Ember 提供一组适合 Redshift 渲染的调色板，强调色盲友好与感知一致性，帮助数据可视化设计者避免误导性配色并快速选用现成配色。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/onecli/onecli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: OneCLI (YC S26) – OSS sandboxed agent harness for teams</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI 开源沙箱代理框架面向团队</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OneCLI is an open-source sandboxed agent harness designed for teams to orchestrate and govern multiple AI agents safely.</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI 是一个面向团队的开源沙箱代理框架，用于安全地编排多代理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OneCLI is an open-source sandboxed agent harness designed for teams to orchestrate and govern multiple AI agents safely. It provides isolation, audit logs, and governance features to reduce risk when using autonomous agents in collaborative workflows. This launch signals growing demand for team-friendly, auditable AI tooling in early-stage ecosystems like YC S26.</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI 是一个面向团队的开源沙箱代理框架，用于安全地编排多代理。它提供隔离、审计日志和治理等功能，降低在协作工作流中使用自主代理的风险。此次发布反映了团队友好、可审计的 AI 工具在初创生态中的需求增长。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/Kazade/picophysics" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Picophysics: Single file physics for games on platforms like N64, PSX, DC</span><span class='zh'>Picophysics：适用于 N64、PSX、Dreamcast 等平台的单文件物理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Picophysics is a compact, single-file physics engine designed for constrained hardware (N64, PSX, Dreamcast).</span><span class='zh'>Picophysics 是面向资源受限硬件（如 N64、PSX、Dreamcast）的单文件物理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Picophysics is a compact, single-file physics engine designed for constrained hardware (N64, PSX, Dreamcast). It emphasizes portability and determinism, helping indie developers add physics to retro-style games with minimal dependencies. The project demonstrates that a tiny footprint can still deliver believable simulations.</span><span class='zh'>Picophysics 是面向资源受限硬件（如 N64、PSX、Dreamcast）的单文件物理引擎。它强调可移植性和确定性，帮助独立开发者在复古风格游戏中加入物理效果，且无需额外依赖。该项目展示了极简实现也能实现可信的物理仿真。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/repsych" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Psychological Warfare in Reverse Engineering (2015)</span><span class='zh'>逆向工程中的心理战（2015）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2015 examination of the psychology involved in reverse engineering, exploring how attackers exploit cognitive biases and social dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇关于逆向工程中心理战的研究，探讨攻击者如何利用认知偏差和社交因素影响防守。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2015 examination of the psychology involved in reverse engineering, exploring how attackers exploit cognitive biases and social dynamics. The work highlights the human element in security research and why technical tools alone aren’t enough.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇关于逆向工程中心理战的研究，探讨攻击者如何利用认知偏差和社交因素影响防守。强调安全研究中的人因要素，单靠工具不足以应对威胁。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critical vulnerability in KVM/x86 enables guest VMs to escape to the host, compromising virtualization isolation.</span><span class='zh'>KVM/x86 中存在一处关键漏洞，允许来宾虚拟机逃逸到宿主机，威胁虚拟化隔离。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critical vulnerability in KVM/x86 enables guest VMs to escape to the host, compromising virtualization isolation. It underscores urgent need for patches and defense-in-depth in virtualization environments.</span><span class='zh'>KVM/x86 中存在一处关键漏洞，允许来宾虚拟机逃逸到宿主机，威胁虚拟化隔离。强调需要紧急修补与加强虚拟化环境的多层防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/yaroslav/kino" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kino: A high-performance Ractor web server for Ruby 4.0</span><span class='zh'>Kino：面向 Ruby 4.0 的高性能 Ractor Web 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kino is a high-performance web server for Ruby 4.0 that leverages the Ractor model for concurrent workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Kino 是面向 Ruby 4.0 的高性能 Web 服务器，利用 Ractor 模型实现并发工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kino is a high-performance web server for Ruby 4.0 that leverages the Ractor model for concurrent workloads. It demonstrates how Ruby’s concurrency tools can scale web apps without heavy frameworks, signaling a potential shift for Ruby deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Kino 是面向 Ruby 4.0 的高性能 Web 服务器，利用 Ractor 模型实现并发工作负载。其展示了 Ruby 的并发工具在无重框架的情况下也能扩展网页应用，可能促使 Ruby 部署方式的转变。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/activeing123/mcptoon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mcptoon – Token-efficient MCP CLI client</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Mcptoon – 令牌高效的 MCP CLI 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mcptoon is a token-efficient MCP CLI client designed for lean, scripting-friendly use.</span><span class='zh'>Mcptoon 是一个令牌高效的 MCP CLI 客户端，设计用于精简、脚本化的命令行使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mcptoon is a token-efficient MCP CLI client designed for lean, scripting-friendly use. It emphasizes compact command-line interactions and careful token handling to minimize costs on MCP services. It illustrates a broader trend toward more economical CLI tooling in API-driven workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Mcptoon 是一个令牌高效的 MCP CLI 客户端，设计用于精简、脚本化的命令行使用。它强调紧凑的交互和对令牌的严格控制，以降低 MCP 服务的成本。体现了在 API 驱动工作流中对高效 CLI 工具的持续追求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/morishuz/delaunay32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Exact, parallel 2D Delaunay triangulation for int32 coordinates</span><span class='zh'>整数坐标的精确并行二维 Delaunay 三角剖分</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A library delivering exact, parallel 2D Delaunay triangulation for int32 coordinates, enabling deterministic geometry computations with integer inputs.</span><span class='zh'>该库提供对 int32 坐标的二维 Delaunay 三角剖分的精确并行实现，确保输入为整数时的确定性几何计算。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A library delivering exact, parallel 2D Delaunay triangulation for int32 coordinates, enabling deterministic geometry computations with integer inputs. It’s valuable for graphics, GIS, and computational geometry workloads that demand reproducible results.</span><span class='zh'>该库提供对 int32 坐标的二维 Delaunay 三角剖分的精确并行实现，确保输入为整数时的确定性几何计算。对需要可重复结果的图形、地理信息系统和计算几何工作负载尤为有用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/runvendo/vendo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Vendo (YC S26) – Let users build features on top of your product</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Vendo（YC S26）——让用户在你的产品上构建自定义功能</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vendo enables developers to let users build features on top of a product, expanding ecosystems without forking.</span><span class='zh'>Vendo 让开发者将能力对外暴露，允许用户在产品之上构建自定义功能，扩展生态系统而无需分叉。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vendo enables developers to let users build features on top of a product, expanding ecosystems without forking. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cesarblum/sectorforth" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sectorforth is a 16-bit x86 Forth that fits in a 512-byte boot sector (2020)</span><span class='zh'>Sectorforth：一个能放入512字节引导扇区的16位 x86 Forth 实现（2020）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sectorforth is a 16-bit x86 Forth that fits in a 512-byte boot sector (2020).</span><span class='zh'>Sectorforth 是一个能放入512字节引导扇区的16位 x86 Forth 实现，展示了极端代码高尔夫和自我托管在极简硬件上的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sectorforth is a 16-bit x86 Forth that fits in a 512-byte boot sector (2020). It demonstrates extreme code golf and self-hosting on minimal hardware, a charming exploration of retro computing and education.</span><span class='zh'>Sectorforth 是一个能放入512字节引导扇区的16位 x86 Forth 实现，展示了极端代码高尔夫和自我托管在极简硬件上的可能性。这是对复古计算与教育价值的有趣探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aravpanwar/decayfmt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decayfmt – a file format that corrupts itself a little every time you open it</span><span class='zh'>Decayfmt——每次打开就会让文件格式自我腐蚀一点点</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Decayfmt is a self-erasing file format that slightly corrupts itself on each open, a provocative concept for exploring data integrity, entropy, and resilience.</span><span class='zh'>Decayfmt 是一种在每次打开时会自我微量腐蚀的文件格式，为数据完整性、熵与鲁棒性提供了一个有趣的思考实验，探讨格式设计与安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Decayfmt is a self-erasing file format that slightly corrupts itself on each open, a provocative concept for exploring data integrity, entropy, and resilience. It serves as a thought experiment in format design and security.</span><span class='zh'>Decayfmt 是一种在每次打开时会自我微量腐蚀的文件格式，为数据完整性、熵与鲁棒性提供了一个有趣的思考实验，探讨格式设计与安全性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agent Lightning v1.0 marks the initial release of a lightweight AI-agent framework designed for building modular, pluggable agents.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Lightning v1.0 作为轻量级 AI 代理框架的初次发布，目标是让构建可模块化的代理更简单。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agent Lightning v1.0 marks the initial release of a lightweight AI-agent framework designed for building modular, pluggable agents. It focuses on simplicity and rapid experimentation, enabling developers to compose skills and integrations with clear interfaces. The release signals an accessible entry point for teams prototyping autonomous workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Lightning v1.0 作为轻量级 AI 代理框架的初次发布，目标是让构建可模块化的代理更简单。它强调简易性和快速试验，开发者可以用清晰的接口组合技能和集成。此发布为团队原型化自治工作流提供了更易入门的入口。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/yegors/deGDID" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Deletes all instances of Microsoft&#x27;s GDID and prevents minting of new ones</span><span class='zh'>删除所有 GDID 实例并阻止新铸造</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A small tool deletes all GDID instances and blocks new minting, highlighting risks around centralized identifiers and the importance of verifiable provenance in software ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>这个工具删除所有 GDID 实例并阻止新的铸造，凸显身份标识治理与可溯性的重要性，也提醒注意对第三方系统的依赖风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A small tool deletes all GDID instances and blocks new minting, highlighting risks around centralized identifiers and the importance of verifiable provenance in software ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>这个工具删除所有 GDID 实例并阻止新的铸造，凸显身份标识治理与可溯性的重要性，也提醒注意对第三方系统的依赖风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/fugue-labs/monty-go" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>monty-go: Pure-Go wrapper for Pydantic&#x27;s Monty Python Interpreter</span><span class='zh'>monty-go：Pydantic Monty Python 解释器的纯 Go 封装</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/uber/submitqueue/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Uber SubmitQueue: a high-performance speculative merge queue</span><span class='zh'>Uber SubmitQueue：高性能的猜测式合并队列</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces a high-performance queue that uses speculative merging to reduce tail latency in large-scale data processing.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一个高性能的提交队列，利用猜测式合并来降低大规模数据处理中尾部延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces a high-performance queue that uses speculative merging to reduce tail latency in large-scale data processing. The design demonstrates how speculative execution and careful concurrency enable faster, more predictable merges under heavy load.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一个高性能的提交队列，利用猜测式合并来降低大规模数据处理中尾部延迟。通过对并发的精细控制和推测合并，在高负载场景下实现更快、可预测的合并效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/161441" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rust 1.98 got a P-critical miscompilation</span><span class='zh'>Rust 1.98 遇到 P-critical 误编译</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A P-critical miscompilation in Rust 1.98 has been reported, highlighting how compiler bugs can affect safety and performance guarantees.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 1.98 出现 P-critical 误编译的报告，暴露编译器漏洞可能影响安全性与性能保证。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A P-critical miscompilation in Rust 1.98 has been reported, highlighting how compiler bugs can affect safety and performance guarantees. It underscores the need for cautious upgrading and monitoring compiler advisories as language ecosystems mature.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 1.98 出现 P-critical 误编译的报告，暴露编译器漏洞可能影响安全性与性能保证。提醒开发者在升级时要谨慎，关注编译器公告与修复进展。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Decimen Optical Transfer introduces a method to transfer files using optical signals encoded with fountain codes into QR codes.</span><span class='zh'>Decimen Optical Transfer 提出通过光学信号与 fountain 编码结合，将文件以 QR 形式传输的方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Decimen Optical Transfer introduces a method to transfer files using optical signals encoded with fountain codes into QR codes. It highlights offline, visual data transfer possibilities and resilience to link instability.</span><span class='zh'>Decimen Optical Transfer 提出通过光学信号与 fountain 编码结合，将文件以 QR 形式传输的方案。强调离线、可视化数据传输的可能性以及对网络不良环境的鲁棒性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/peterkelly/rex" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Rex, a parallel functional language for scientific workflows</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Rex，一种用于科学工作流的并行函数式语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rex is a parallel functional language designed for scientific workflows, enabling data-parallel pipelines to run efficiently.</span><span class='zh'>Rex 是面向科学工作流的并行函数式语言，旨在高效运行数据并行的工作流管道，简化复杂科学分析的组合、调度与扩展，并提供强保证。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rex is a parallel functional language designed for scientific workflows, enabling data-parallel pipelines to run efficiently. It aims to simplify composing, scheduling, and scaling complex scientific analyses with strong guarantees.</span><span class='zh'>Rex 是面向科学工作流的并行函数式语言，旨在高效运行数据并行的工作流管道，简化复杂科学分析的组合、调度与扩展，并提供强保证。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tool for diagnosing unstable Python tests helps identify root causes of flakiness, using traces and reproducibility techniques to guide fixes.</span><span class='zh'>一个用于诊断不稳定 Python 测试的开源工具，帮助工程师通过追踪和复现性分析找出非确定性失败的根本原因，从而加速调试与 CI 流水线的可靠性提升。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zcomplete is a shell plugin that automatically corrects typos in shell commands, helping users recover from mistyped commands and speed up workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Zcomplete 是一个 Shell 插件，能够自动纠正命令输入中的错字，帮助用户从打错的命令中快速恢复并提升工作效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zcomplete is a shell plugin that automatically corrects typos in shell commands, helping users recover from mistyped commands and speed up workflows. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paveluv/e" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>e is a customizable self-aware Emacs-like editor written in Chez Scheme</span><span class='zh'>e：用 Chez Scheme 编写、可定制且具自我意识的 Emacs 风格编辑器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>e is a customizable, self-aware Emacs-like editor written in Chez Scheme.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用 Chez Scheme 编写、可高度定制、具自我意识特性的 Emacs 风格编辑器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>e is a customizable, self-aware Emacs-like editor written in Chez Scheme. The design leans on Scheme&#x27;s metaprogramming and introspection to let users reshape the editor at runtime, potentially blurring lines between editor and environment. It serves as a playful showcase of how a Lisp-based tool can be deeply adapted to individual workflows.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用 Chez Scheme 编写、可高度定制、具自我意识特性的 Emacs 风格编辑器。依托 Scheme 的元编程与自省能力，用户可以在运行时深度改造编辑器的行为与工作流，展示了 Lisp 语言在编辑器领域的深度定制潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Kayvan-Zahiri/asr-age-gap" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Whisper transcribes 70-year-olds more accurately than 20-year-olds</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Whisper 在转录中对70岁人群比20岁更准确</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN post reveals that Whisper&#x27;s transcription accuracy is higher for older voices (70-year-olds) than for younger ones (20-year-olds).</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Whisper 的转录对70岁群体的准确度高于20岁群体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN post reveals that Whisper&#x27;s transcription accuracy is higher for older voices (70-year-olds) than for younger ones (20-year-olds). The finding highlights age-related biases in speech recognition models and raises questions about training data diversity and accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Whisper 的转录对70岁群体的准确度高于20岁群体。这一发现揭示了语音识别模型的年龄偏差，并引发对训练数据多样性和无障碍性的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/ziglang/zig/pulls/36633" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zig to remove support for wasi-libc</span><span class='zh'>Zig 将移除对 wasi-libc 的支持</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zig language project plans to drop wasi-libc compatibility, affecting WebAssembly cross-platform projects.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 语言计划移除对 wasi-libc 的兼容性，影响基于 WebAssembly 的跨平台项目。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zig language project plans to drop wasi-libc compatibility, affecting WebAssembly cross-platform projects. The move may simplify the toolchain but requires developers to adapt to alternative WASI setups.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 语言计划移除对 wasi-libc 的兼容性，影响基于 WebAssembly 的跨平台项目。此举或简化工具链，但开发者需调整到替代的 WASI 配置。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kostey/khms-memory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>KHMS – a file-based long-term memory an LLM agent installs into itself</span><span class='zh'>KHMS：LLM 自身安装的基于文件的长期记忆</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Describes KHMS, a memory system that stores long-term memory for an LLM agent in files, enabling persistency beyond session scope.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 KHMS，这是一种把长期记忆以文件形式存储在 LLM 代理中的系统，使记忆在会话之外也可持久化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes KHMS, a memory system that stores long-term memory for an LLM agent in files, enabling persistency beyond session scope. It discusses architecture, retrieval, and safety considerations.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 KHMS，这是一种把长期记忆以文件形式存储在 LLM 代理中的系统，使记忆在会话之外也可持久化。讨论其架构、检索机制与安全性等考量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/YuriKovalov22/the-finn" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Finn – an agent that lives in my router and complains about it</span><span class='zh'>The Finn：一个驻留在我的路由器里、对路由器发牢骚的智能体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A lightweight edge AI project, The Finn runs on a home router to monitor network health and provide feedback.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个在家用路由器上运行的边缘智能体原型，用于监控网络健康并提供反馈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A lightweight edge AI project, The Finn runs on a home router to monitor network health and provide feedback. It playfully critiques the router while illustrating what tiny on-device agents can do for observability and user experience.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个在家用路由器上运行的边缘智能体原型，用于监控网络健康并提供反馈。它以幽默的方式对路由器进行吐槽，同时展示了小型在设备上运行的代理在可观测性和用户体验上的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/harlixay7/ReclaimArc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I ran out of space extracting a RAR so I built ReclaimArc</span><span class='zh'>我在提取 RAR 时空间用尽，于是我构建 ReclaimArc</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shares a personal project, ReclaimArc, built after exhausting disk space while extracting a RAR archive.</span><span class='zh'>讲述作者在提取 RAR 文件时空间用尽的经历，随之开发了 ReclaimArc。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shares a personal project, ReclaimArc, built after exhausting disk space while extracting a RAR archive. It describes how the tool streamlines reclaiming space, maybe by deduping, compressing, or reallocating storage.</span><span class='zh'>讲述作者在提取 RAR 文件时空间用尽的经历，随之开发了 ReclaimArc。介绍工具如何高效回收、整理磁盘空间，以及实现要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ducasse/SmalltalkReportPDFArchive" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smalltalk Report from 1991 to 1996</span><span class='zh'>Smalltalk 报告：1991–1996 年</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective glimpse into the Smalltalk ecosystem from 1991–1996, highlighting language design choices, VM work, and how that era influenced modern languages and tooling.</span><span class='zh'>对 Smalltalk 1991–1996 年间的生态进行回顾，聚焦语言设计、虚拟机实现以及对现代语言与工具的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective glimpse into the Smalltalk ecosystem from 1991–1996, highlighting language design choices, VM work, and how that era influenced modern languages and tooling.</span><span class='zh'>对 Smalltalk 1991–1996 年间的生态进行回顾，聚焦语言设计、虚拟机实现以及对现代语言与工具的影响。</span></p>
